Patch 6.0.6¶

What changed in the game data from 6.0.5 to 6.0.6, found by comparing the two versions. Struck text is gone, marked text is new. Only the data is compared: a change to how the code works doesn't show here.

Passive Tree¶

Changed (3)¶

Name Type Change
Troll's Fist, Fenring's Claw, Stout Fist Notable Dash Punch: Fist → Charged fist attacks dash towards the aimed target within 7 m; Without an aimed target, charged fist attacks step 2 m forward; Fist → Charged fist and unarmed attacks dash towards the aimed target, stopping at striking range when the hit is released. Obstacles stop the dash; area modifiers do not increase its range.

Currency¶

Changed (1)¶

Name Change
Great Rune of First Essence Randomises the numeric values of an item's implicit properties. On a room of the halls, rerolls its type; the biome stays. Take a single room out of a stack first.

Monster Modifiers¶

Changed (1)¶

Name Change
Deathward Links up to 5 creatures near it. While it lives, the creatures linked to it → they cannot fall more than 15% of health below the healthiest of them.

Keywords¶

Changed (2)¶

Name Change
Fist dash Moves the character toward the aimed target before a charged fist attack, stopping at striking distance. With no target it travels forward a shorter distance; area of effect scales → does the → not dash → change its range.
Rune shield A protective layer in front of health: it takes only the damage left after every reduction (resistances, block, armour and the rest). Spirit and poison damage bypass it. After a pause without damage, the shield recharges; recharge delay and regeneration control this recovery. A hit you evade interrupts the recharge the same way taking damage does.
